> Academic Research Skills for Claude Code is a Claude Code/CLI plugin that delivers an end-to-end toolkit for research workflows: planning, literature retrieval, citation locating, writing calibration, review, and finalization. It emphasizes provenance and claim-level auditability through trust-chain frontmatter and three-layer citation anchors, plus an optional claim-audit pass and multi-stage integrity gates to flag hallucinations, fabricated references, or unsupported claims. Features include style calibration, writing-quality checks, and reviewer-mode metrics. Designed for human-in-the-loop researchers, it automates verification and formatting while preserving researcher judgment.

> SmartProxy is a high-performance, cross-platform proxy manager written in Go that unifies multiple network interfaces (Company VPN, Personal VPN/GFW, Direct) via domain-based intelligent routing. It automatically directs corporate domains to a company VPN, routes blocked domains using a gfwlist to a personal VPN, and keeps local traffic direct. Features include a responsive Bootstrap web GUI, system tray controls, real-time connection logging, JSON configuration, single-instance locking, and randomized GUI port to avoid conflicts. Provides build scripts and installer support for macOS and Windows deployment.
> <sub>A high-performance utility for managing proxy configurations, written in Go to ensure speed and reliability.</sub>

> Humanizer is a Claude Code/OpenCode skill that detects and removes signs of AI-generated writing to make text sound more natural. > PaperBanana is an open-source, reference-driven multi-agent framework that automates conversion of paper method text into publication-quality diagrams and plots. It orchestrates five specialized agents—Retriever, Planner, Stylist, Visualizer, and Critic—using in-context example guidance and iterative refinement to produce semantically accurate, stylistically consistent scientific illustrations. The system integrates vision-language models and state-of-the-art image generators, supports model selection, OpenRouter and Hugging Face Spaces hosting, and targets use cases like paper figures, method flowcharts, and research visualizations to aid reproducibility and rapid diagram prototyping.

> `#Network Proxy` `#Rust` `#Transparent Proxy` 
> mihomo-rust is a high-performance Rust implementation of the mihomo/Clash proxy kernel. It offers rule-based tunneling and multi-protocol outbound support including Shadowsocks, Trojan, VLESS, HTTP, and SOCKS5. The project provides transparent proxy (TProxy) with nftables/pf integration, DNS snooping and caching, TLS privacy features such as ECH and uTLS fingerprinting, a REST API and a built-in web dashboard. Advanced proxy groups (Selector, URLTest, Fallback, LoadBalance, Relay) and a rich rule engine enable precise traffic routing, load distribution and resilient failover for server and edge deployments.

> See-through presents a pipeline that converts a single anime illustration into manipulatable 2.5D assets by decomposing one image into up to 23 fully inpainted, semantically distinct transparent layers with inferred drawing order, producing PSD-like outputs. The approach combines a diffusion-based LayerDiff3D (SDXL) for transparent layer generation, a Marigold pseudo-depth estimator fine-tuned for anime, and semantic segmentation tools (optional SAM/detectron2). The repository includes inference scripts, pretrained models, and annotator options. Applications include character editing, outfit swaps, layered rendering, relighting, and animation asset creation.

> Tree Sandbox is a rootless, single-file Linux sandbox implementing a tree-shaped, multi-layer container model. It allows unlimited nesting of lightweight sub-containers, on-demand bind mounts from the host (files, sockets, devices), optional GUI forwarding (X11, Xpra, isolated compositors), and rootless network namespaces with tun/tap and nftables plus fine-grained localhost port merging. No images, no system daemon, and no subuid/subgid required. It’s designed for layered application isolation, secure testing of untrusted or semi-trusted processes, and hardware passthrough in a minimal, image-free workflow.

> Claude Context is an MCP plugin that provides semantic code search for Claude Code and other AI coding assistants, treating an entire repository as searchable context. It builds vectors using OpenAI embeddings and stores them in a vector database (Milvus/Zilliz Cloud), enabling retrieval of relevant code snippets from millions of lines without loading whole directories into the model. This approach reduces token and compute costs, scales to large codebases, and integrates via Node.js MCP components, npm packages and a VS Code extension. A companion project (memsearch) offers persistent agent memory across sessions.

> Neo is a Chrome extension that turns any web app into a replayable API without relying on official endpoints or brittle browser automation. It passively captures fetch/XHR traffic — URLs, headers, bodies, timing and the DOM trigger — and auto-generates API schemas (endpoints, auth, params, responses, error codes). Calls are executed inside the page via Chrome DevTools Protocol so cookies, CSRF and sessions are inherited; when no API exists Neo drives the UI via an accessibility tree (click/fill/type/press/scroll). Supports headless Lightpanda and a single CLI for AI-driven, robust automation and scraping.
> <sub>Turn any web app into an API. Chrome extension captures browser traffic, auto-generates schemas, lets AI replay APIs directly. No official API needed.</sub>

It provides cross-platform wrappers around child_process, safe argument escaping, sensible defaults, and simple concurrency primitives for running commands. zx supports Node.js, Bun, Deno and GraalVM, and works with Bash or PowerShell environments. It streamlines deployment, build orchestration and DevOps automation by reducing boilerplate and improving portability, readability, and maintainability of complex scripts. It also offers a Promise-based API, argument interpolation, and extensive examples and docs.

> anylinuxfs enables full read-write mounting of Linux-native filesystems (ext4, btrfs, XFS, ZFS, NTFS, exFAT, plus LUKS/LVM/RAID) on macOS by running a lightweight libkrun microVM per volume and exposing the filesystem over NFS. It avoids kernel extensions or macFUSE, supports disk images, encrypted volumes (LUKS/BitLocker) and multi-disk setups. CLI-focused and Apple Silicon–only, it trades a small VM per mount for native Linux filesystem fidelity, useful for recovery, cross-platform development and direct access to Linux disks from a Mac.
> <sub>macOS: mount any linux-supported filesystem read/write using NFS and a microVM</sub>

It supports sources such as Docker Hub, gcr.io, k8s.io and ghcr.io, handles images up to 40GB, multi-architecture images via --platform, namespace conflict resolution, and scheduled syncs. > Angie is a high-performance, nginx-forked drop-in web server compatible with existing nginx configurations and modules. It advances nginx by providing native HTTP/3 for client and upstream connections, built-in ACME for automated TLS issuance (HTTP/DNS/ALPN), a RESTful JSON API for configuration and metrics, and customizable Prometheus exports. Angie also simplifies location matching and exposes observability via a lightweight console, making it suitable for production reverse-proxy, edge TLS, and performance-sensitive deployments that require modern protocol support and backward compatibility.

> Nature is a general-purpose systems programming language and compiler focused on high performance, memory safety, and concise syntax. It features a strong static type system with generics, enums/tagged unions, interfaces, nullable/errable types and built-in concurrency primitives (go/future/channel/select). Nature compiles directly to machine code without relying on LLVM, supports cross-compilation and static musl linking, and provides zero-overhead C FFI. Its custom runtime implements a low‑pause GC, tcmalloc-style allocator, shared‑stack coroutines and libuv-based IO. Bundled tools include npkg, LSP, formatter and in-source testing—suited for network services, system tools and high‑perf libraries.
> <sub>The Nature Programming Language</sub>

> This repository provides an LD_PRELOAD hook library to enable Tencent Wemeet screen sharing on Wayland without using a virtual camera. It intercepts XShmAttach/XShmGetImage/XShmDetach, spoofs XDamage, launches xdg-desktop-portal and PipeWire threads, and writes frames into a framebuffer so Wemeet can capture the screen. It supports KDE, GNOME, Hyprland and many wlroots compositors, with Arch AUR and Flatpak usage notes. Note: upstream Wemeet now supports Wayland natively; this project is archived and users should upgrade to the official client.

> Proxy Protocol Support is a server-side mod for Fabric and Quilt that adds HAProxy Proxy Protocol parsing to Minecraft servers. It allows handling proxied and direct connections simultaneously while exposing players' real IPs when forwarded, useful with TCPShield, Nginx, FRP and similar TCP proxies. The mod enforces a secure 'default deny' model with configurable trusted proxy and direct-access IP/CIDR whitelists, optional automatic TCPShield IP fetching, and settings in config/proxy_protocol_support.json. Server-side only; supports Minecraft 1.14–1.21.10 and likely newer versions. Ideal for operators who want to obfuscate server IPs without losing accurate source addressing.
> <sub>Proxy Protocol support for Fabric and Quilt servers</sub>

> `#Networking` `#Port Hopping` `#STUN/NAT Traversal` `#UDP` 
> UDP Hop is a tool designed to bypass consumer broadband UDP throttling by periodically re-establishing UDP connections and migrating traffic across new source IP/port tuples. It preserves native UDP (no TCP encapsulation), assigns Session IDs to distinguish flows, and expires idle sessions automatically. Server mode supports STUN hole punching and both IPv4 and IPv6. Configurable options include port ranges, scheduled port refresh, FEC and multiple AEAD ciphers. UDP Hop targets deployment on home routers or soft-routers to maintain higher UDP throughput via port/IP hopping.

## dndx/phantun

> [!info]
> ⭐ 2,331 · Rust · 2026-05-19T03:46:19Z  
> [GitHub](https://github.com/dndx/phantun)  
> `#Firewall/NAT Traversal` `#Rust` `#Tunneling` 
> Phantun is a lightweight, high-performance UDP-to-TCP obfuscator written in safe Rust. It encapsulates UDP packet streams into fake TCP connections designed to traverse L3/L4 stateful and stateless firewalls and NATs while preserving UDP semantics such as out-of-order delivery and avoiding TCP-style retransmission and flow control. Phantun creates TUN interfaces on client and server, is optimized for multi-core scaling, and minimizes encapsulation overhead. It’s ideal when UDP is blocked or throttled but TCP is permitted, providing low-latency tunneling without L7 proxy compatibility.
> <sub>Transforms UDP stream into (fake) TCP streams that can go through Layer 3 & Layer 4 (NAPT) firewalls/NATs.</sub>

> [GitHub](https://github.com/LaiJingli/rmtrash)  
> `#Command-line Recycle Bin` `#Shell Script` `#System Utilities` 
> rmtrash is a lightweight shell wrapper that transparently replaces the system rm on Linux and macOS, moving removed files to a recoverable recycle bin instead of permanently deleting them. It preserves deletion history and provides commands to list, restore, delete-by-name and empty the bin. It supports common rm flags (-r, -f, -fr) while guarding against dangerous operations (e.g. rejecting rm -fr /). Installation is simple (wget, move, chmod) and can be enabled per-user or globally via an alias; use /bin/rm to bypass the trash for permanent removal.
> <sub>rmtrash 是linux和mac下命令行版本rm的回收站，安装后对用户透明，符合正常使用rm的习惯(支持rm -fr file哦)，有了他再也不怕rm时候手颤抖了(能自动拒绝 rm -fr / 哦)。 rmtrash stands for "rm trash" which acts just like the system built-in rm command,and just moves the file to the trash for recovery when needed.</sub>

Backed by an active community and LGPL components, it offers a stable, high-performance foundation for C++ modders targeting Bedrock servers and clients.
> <sub>A lightweight, modular and versatile mod loader for Minecraft Bedrock Edition, formerly known as LiteLoaderBDS</sub>

> Ventoy is an open-source bootable USB solution that lets you boot ISO/WIM/IMG/VHD(x)/EFI files simply by copying them to a USB drive—no repeated reformatting. It supports Legacy BIOS and multiple UEFI architectures (x86, x86_64, ARM64, MIPS64) as well as MBR and GPT partition styles. Ventoy presents a boot menu for multiple images, supports browsing local images, and is tested with 1300+ ISOs covering most Linux distributions, Windows and rescue tools—ideal for installation, deployment, maintenance and recovery workflows.

> `#Game Tools` `#Rust` `#Slime Chunk Detection` 
> SlimeChunkFinder is a high-performance Rust command-line tool for Minecraft Bedrock Edition that locates multi-slime spawning chunks across massive areas. It can scan billions of chunks—reportedly over 3.5 billion on a high-end CPU—completing comprehensive border searches in minutes. The tool supports arbitrary-shaped query regions and configurable mob spawn radii to emulate different spawn ranges. It's aimed at server operators, seed analysts and map planners who need deterministic slime-chunk detection at scale. Build requires Rust; tweak parameters in main and run cargo run --release.
